<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:34; 1 Chronicles 6:25–27; 1 Samuel 1:19"> Now there was a certain man of Ramatayim-Tzofim &#91;double height of the watchers&#93;, of mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and his name &#91;was&#93; Elkanah &#91;God Has Taken Possession&#93;, the son of Yerocham &#91;showing pity&#93;, the son of Elihu &#91;He is my God&#93;, the son of Tochu &#91;lowly&#93;, the son of Tzuf &#91;honeycomb&#93;, an Efrat &#91;ashiness: fruitfulness&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 16:1–2; Judges 13:2; Genesis 29:23–29"> And he had two wives; the name of the one &#91;was&#93; Hannah &#91;grace&#93;, and the name of the other P’ninah &#91;jewel&#93;: and P’ninah &#91;jewel&#93; had children, but Hannah &#91;grace&#93; had no children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Luke 2:41; Joshua 18:1; Deuteronomy 12:5–7"> And this man went up out of his city yearly to worship and to sacrifice unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; in Shiloh &#91;Place of Rest&#93;. <span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 24:26; Genesis 24:52; 2 Timothy 3:15"> Then answered one of the people, and said, Your1s father straitly charged the people with an oath, saying, Cursed &#91;be&#93; the man that eats &#91;any&#93; food this day. And the people were faint.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Then said Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93;, My father has troubled the land: see, I pray you, how my eyes have been enlightened, because I tasted a little of this honey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And they struck the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; that day from Mikhmas &#91;hidden&#93; to Ayalon &#91;Field of Deer&#93;: and the people were very faint.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And he gathered an host, and struck the ‘Amalekim &#91;Dwellers in a Valley&#93;, and delivered Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of the hands of them that spoiled them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> Now the sons of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; were Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93;, and Yishvi &#91;he resembles me&#93;, and Malkishua &#91;my king is wealth&#93;: and the names of his two daughters &#91;were these&#93;; the name of the firstborn Merav &#91;increase&#93;, and the name of the younger Mikhal &#91;who is like God?&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> And the name of Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; wife &#91;was&#93; Achino’am &#91;my brother is delight&#93;, the daughter of Achima’atz &#91;my brother is anger (wrath)&#93;: and the name of the captain of his host &#91;was&#93; Avner &#91;my father is a lamp&#93;, the son of Ner &#91;lamp&#93;, Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; uncle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> And there was sore war against the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; all the days of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;: and when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; saw any strong man, or any valiant man, he took him unto him.</span>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="1 Thessalonians 5:17; Jacob 5:16; Luke 18:1"> Now David &#91;beloved&#93; &#91;was&#93; the son of that Efrat &#91;ashiness: fruitfulness&#93; of Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93; in Y’hudah, whose name &#91;was&#93; Yishai &#91;I possess&#93;; and he had eight sons: and the man went among men &#91;for&#93; an old man in the days of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Romans 8:26; Acts 2:13; Genesis 24:42–45"> And the three eldest sons of Yishai &#91;I possess&#93; went &#91;and&#93; followed Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; to the battle: and the names of his three sons that went to the battle &#91;were&#93; ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93; the firstborn, and next unto him Avinadav &#91;my father is noble&#93;, and the third Shammah &#91;astonishment&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Ephesians 4:25; Job 8:2; Mishlei - Proverbs 6:9"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; &#91;was&#93; the youngest: and the three eldest followed Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:12; Job 6:2–3; Gosel 12:34–35"> And the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; drew near morning and evening, and presented himself forty days.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Mark 5:34; Judges 18:6; Tehillim - Psalms 20:3–5"> And Yishai &#91;I possess&#93; said unto David &#91;beloved&#93; his son, Take now for youi brethren an eifah of this parched &#91;corn&#93;, and these ten loaves, and run to the camp to youi brethren;</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ruth 2:13; Romans 15:13; Ecclesiastes 9:7"> And carry these ten cheeses unto the captain of &#91;their&#93; thousand, and look how youi brethren fare, and take their pledge.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 21:1; Genesis 30:22; 1 Samuel 1:11"> Now Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and they, and all the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;were&#93; in the valley of Elah &#91;an oak&#93;, fighting with the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 41:51–52; Gosel 1:21; Exodus 2:10"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; rose up early in the morning, and left the sheep with a keeper, and took, and went, as Yishai &#91;I possess&#93; had commanded him; and he came to the trench, as the host was going forth to the fight, and shouted for the battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Samuel 1:3; Deuteronomy 12:11; Joshua 24:15"> For Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; had put the battle in array (to put on; to clothe), army against army.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="1 Samuel 1:28; 1 Samuel 1:11; Luke 2:22"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; left his carriage (baggage) in the hand of the keeper of the carriage (baggage), and ran into the army, and came and saluted his brethren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:25; Luke 11:27; Genesis 21:7–8"> And as he talked with them, behold, there came up the champion, the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; of Gat &#91;Winepress&#93;, Golyat &#91;splendor&#93; by name, out of the armies of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and spoke according to the same words: and David &#91;beloved&#93; heard &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 15:9–10; Joshua 18:1; 1 Samuel 4:3–4"> And all the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when they saw the man, fled from him, and were sore afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 18:15–16; Luke 2:22"> And the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; said, Have you2f seen this man that is come up? surely to defy Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; is he come up: and it shall be, &#91;that&#93; the man who kills him, the king will enrich him with great riches, and will give him his daughter, and make his father’s house free in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="2 Kings 4:30; 2 Kings 2:4; 2 Kings 2:6"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; spoke to the men that stood by him, saying, What shall be done to the man that kills this P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, and takes away the reproach (disgrace; shame) from Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;? for who &#91;is&#93; this uncircumcised P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, that he should defy the armies of the living God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="1 John 5:15; Tehillim - Psalms 118:5; Gosel 7:7"> And the people answered him after this manner, saying, So shall it be done to the man that kills him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 24:26; Genesis 24:52; 2 Timothy 3:15"> And ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93; his eldest brother heard when he spoke unto the men; and ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93;’s anger was kindled against David &#91;beloved&#93;, and he said, Why came youi down here? and with whom have youi left those few sheep in the wilderness? I know youi pride, and the naughtiness of youi heart; for youi are come down that youi might see the battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said, What have I now done? &#91;Is there&#93; not a cause?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And he turned from him toward another, and spoke after the same manner: and the people answered him again after the former manner.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And when the words were heard which David &#91;beloved&#93; spoke, they rehearsed &#91;them&#93; before Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;: and he sent for him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said to Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, Let no man’s heart fail because of him; youi servant will go and fight with this P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said to David &#91;beloved&#93;, youi are not able to go against this P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; to fight with him: for youi &#91;are but&#93; a youth, and he a man of war from his youth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said unto Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, You1s servant kept his father’s sheep, and there came a lion, and a bear, and took a lamb out of the flock:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And I went out after him, and struck him, and delivered &#91;it&#93; out of his mouth: and when he arose against me, I caught &#91;him&#93; by his beard, and struck him, and killed him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> You1s servant killed both the lion and the bear: and this uncircumcised P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; shall be as one of them, seeing he has defied the armies of the living God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> David &#91;beloved&#93; said moreover, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that delivered me out of the paw of the lion, and out of the paw of the bear, he will deliver me out of the hand of this P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;. And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said unto David &#91;beloved&#93;, Go, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) be with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; armed David &#91;beloved&#93; with his armour, and he put an helmet of brass upon his head; also he armed him with a coat of mail.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; girded his sword upon his armour, and he assayed (attempted; tested; analyzed) to go; for he had not proved &#91;it&#93;. And David &#91;beloved&#93; said unto Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, I cannot go with these; for I have not proved &#91;them&#93;. And David &#91;beloved&#93; put them off him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And he took his staff in his hand, and chose him five smooth stones out of the brook, and put them in a shepherd’s bag which he had, even in a scrip; and his sling &#91;was&#93; in his hand: and he drew near to the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; came on and drew near unto David &#91;beloved&#93;; and the man that bore the shield &#91;went&#93; before him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And when the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; looked about, and saw David &#91;beloved&#93;, he disdained him: for he was &#91;but&#93; a youth, and ruddy (reddish hair or complexion), and of a fair Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; said unto David &#91;beloved&#93;, &#91;Am&#93; I a dog, that youi come to me with rods (clubs)? And the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; cursed David &#91;beloved&#93; by his gods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; said to David &#91;beloved&#93;, Come to me, and I will give youi flesh unto the fowls of the air, and to the beasts of the field.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> Then said David &#91;beloved&#93; to the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, youi come to me with a sword, and with a spear, and with a shield: but I come to youi in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of the armies of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, whom youi have defied.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> This day will the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) deliver youi into my hand; and I will strike youi, and take youi head from youi; and I will give the carcasses of the host of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; this day unto the fowls of the air, and to the wild beasts of the earth; that all the earth may know that there is a God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And all this assembly shall know that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) saves not with sword and spear: for the battle &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and he will give you into our hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And it came to pass, when the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; arose, and came and drew near to meet David &#91;beloved&#93;, that David &#91;beloved&#93; hasted, and ran toward the army to meet the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; put his hand in his bag, and took there a stone, and slang &#91;it&#93;, and struck the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; in his forehead, that the stone sunk into his forehead; and he fell upon his face to the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> So David &#91;beloved&#93; prevailed over the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; with a sling and with a stone, and struck the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, and killed him; but &#91;there was&#93; no sword in the hand of David &#91;beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> Therefore David &#91;beloved&#93; ran, and stood upon the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, and took his sword, and drew it out of the sheath thereof, and killed him, and cut off his head with that. And when the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; saw their champion was dead, they fled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> And the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; arose, and shouted, and pursued the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, until youi come to the valley, and to the gates of ‘Ekron &#91;Torn Up by the Roots&#93;. And the wounded of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; fell down by the way to Sha’arayim &#91;double gate&#93;, even unto Gat &#91;Winepress&#93;, and unto ‘Ekron &#91;Torn Up by the Roots&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; returned from chasing after the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and they spoiled their tents.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; saw David &#91;beloved&#93; go forth against the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, he said unto Avner &#91;my father is a lamp&#93;, the captain of the host, Avner &#91;my father is a lamp&#93;, whose son &#91;is&#93; this youth? And Avner &#91;my father is a lamp&#93; said, &#91;As&#93; youi soul lives, O king, I cannot tell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> And the king said, Enquire youi whose son the stripling &#91;is&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> And as David &#91;beloved&#93; returned from the slaughter of the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, Avner &#91;my father is a lamp&#93; took him, and brought him before Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; with the head of the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93; in his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said to him, Whose son &#91;are&#93; youi, &#91;youi&#93; young man? And David &#91;beloved&#93; answered, I &#91;am&#93; the son of youi servant Yishai &#91;I possess&#93; the Beit-Lachmi &#91;house of bread&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:34; 1 Chronicles 6:25–27; 1 Samuel 1:19"> And it came to pass, when he had made an end of speaking unto Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, that the soul of Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93; was knit with the soul of David &#91;beloved&#93;, and Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93; loved him as his own soul.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 30:2; Genesis 29:30–31; Genesis 45:22"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; went out to wherever Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; sent him, &#91;and&#93; behaved himself wisely: and Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; set him over the men of war, and he was accepted in the sight of all the people, and also in the sight of Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; servants.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Job 6:14; Job 24:21; Leviticus 18:18"> And it came to pass as they came, when David &#91;beloved&#93; was returned from the slaughter of the P’lishtim &#91;immigrants&#93;, that the women came out of all cities of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, singing and dancing, to meet king Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, with tambourines, with joy, and with instruments of music.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ruth 4:15; Job 6:14; Isaiah 54:6"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was very exceedingly angry, and the saying displeased him; nd he said, They have ascribed unto David &#91;beloved&#93; ten thousands, and to me they have ascribed &#91;but&#93; thousands: and &#91;what&#93; can he have more but the kingdom?</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Job 7:11; Luke 22:44; Job 10:1"> And it came to pass on the morrow, that the evil spirit from God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; came upon Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and he prophesied in the midst of the house: and David &#91;beloved&#93; played with his hand, as at other times: and &#91;there was&#93; a javelin in Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Judges 13:5; Numbers 6:5; Genesis 29:32"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; cast the javelin; for he said, I will strike David &#91;beloved&#93; even to the wall &#91;with it&#93;. And David &#91;beloved&#93; avoided out of his presence twice.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="1 Thessalonians 5:17; Jacob 5:16; Luke 18:1"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was afraid of David &#91;beloved&#93;, because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was with him, and was departed from Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Romans 8:26; Acts 2:13; Genesis 24:42–45"> Therefore Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; removed him from him, and made him his captain over a thousand; and he went out and came in before the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Ephesians 4:25; Job 8:2; Mishlei - Proverbs 6:9"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; behaved himself wisely in all his ways; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;was&#93; with him.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:12; Job 6:2–3; Gosel 12:34–35"> But all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; loved David &#91;beloved&#93;, because he went out and came in before them.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Mark 5:34; Judges 18:6; Tehillim - Psalms 20:3–5"> And Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said to David &#91;beloved&#93;, Behold my elder daughter Merav &#91;increase&#93;, her will I give youi to wife: only be youi valiant for me, and fight the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; battles. For Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; said, Let not my hand be upon him, but let the hand of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; be upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ruth 2:13; Romans 15:13; Ecclesiastes 9:7"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said unto Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, Who &#91;am&#93; I? and what &#91;is&#93; my life, &#91;or&#93; my father’s family in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that I should be son in law to the king?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 21:1; Genesis 30:22; 1 Samuel 1:11"> But it came to pass at the time when Merav &#91;increase&#93; Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; daughter should have been given to David &#91;beloved&#93;, that she was given unto Adri’el &#91;flock of God&#93; the Mecholati &#91;dancing (in sadness)&#93; to wife.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 41:51–52; Gosel 1:21; Exodus 2:10"> And Mikhal &#91;who is like God?&#93; Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; daughter loved David &#91;beloved&#93;: and they told Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and the thing pleased him.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Samuel 1:3; Deuteronomy 12:11; Joshua 24:15"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; came to the two hundred men, which were so faint that they could not follow David &#91;beloved&#93;, whom they had made also to abide at the brook B’sor &#91;cheerful&#93;: and they went forth to meet David &#91;beloved&#93;, and to meet the people that &#91;were&#93; with him: and when David &#91;beloved&#93; came near to the people, he saluted them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="1 Samuel 1:28; 1 Samuel 1:11; Luke 2:22"> Then answered all the wicked men and &#91;men&#93; of Belial &#91;Worthless, Good for Nothing&#93;, of those that went with David &#91;beloved&#93;, and said, Because they went not with us, we will not give them &#91;ought&#93; of the spoil (plunder; prey; booty) that we have recovered, save to every man his wife and his children, that they may lead &#91;them&#93; away, and depart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="2 Samuel 7:25; Luke 11:27; Genesis 21:7–8"> Then said David &#91;beloved&#93;, You2f shall not do so, my brethren, with that which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given us, who has preserved us, and delivered the company that came against us into our hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 15:9–10; Joshua 18:1; 1 Samuel 4:3–4"> For who will hearken unto you in this matter? but as his part &#91;is&#93; that goes down to the battle, so &#91;shall&#93; his part &#91;be&#93; that tarries by the stuff: they shall part alike.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 18:15–16; Luke 2:22"> And it was &#91;so&#93; from that day forward, that he made it a statute and an ordinance for Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="2 Kings 4:30; 2 Kings 2:4; 2 Kings 2:6"> And when David &#91;beloved&#93; came to Ziklag &#91;Winding&#93;, he sent of the spoil (plunder; prey; booty) unto the elders of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, &#91;even&#93; to his friends, saying, Behold a present for you of the spoil (plunder; prey; booty) of the enemies of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="1 John 5:15; Tehillim - Psalms 118:5; Gosel 7:7"> To &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Beit-El &#91;House of God&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in south Ramot &#91;Heights&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Yatir &#91;Plenty&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 24:26; Genesis 24:52; 2 Timothy 3:15"> And to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in ‘Aeo’er &#91;Ruins&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Sifmot &#91;fruitful&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Esht’moa &#91;I will make myself heard&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Rakhal &#91;trade&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in the cities of the Yerachme’eli &#91;my God have pity&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in the cities of the Keini &#91;traveling tinkers or blacksmiths&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Hormah &#91;Asylum&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Kor-’Ashan &#91;furnace of smoke&#93;, and to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in ‘Atakh &#91;lodging place&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And to &#91;them&#93; which &#91;were&#93; in Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93;, and to all the places where David &#91;beloved&#93; himself and his men were wont (used to; in the habit of) to haunt (where one goes freequently).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 6:34; 1 Chronicles 6:25–27; 1 Samuel 1:19"> Now the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; fought against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; fled from before the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and fell down slain in mount Gilboa &#91;swollen heap&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 16:1–2; Judges 13:2; Genesis 29:23–29"> And the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; followed hard upon Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and upon his sons; and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; killed Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93;, and Avinadav &#91;my father is noble&#93;, and Malkishua &#91;my king is wealth&#93;, Sha’ul’s &#91;desired&#93; sons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Luke 2:41; Joshua 18:1; Deuteronomy 12:5–7"> And the battle went sore against Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and the archers hit him; and he was sore wounded of the archers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:17–18; Deuteronomy 16:11; Deuteronomy 12:5–7"> Then said Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; unto his armourbearer, Draw youri sword, and thrust me through with that; lest these uncircumcised come and thrust me through, and abuse me. But his armourbearer would not; for he was sore afraid. Therefore Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; took a sword, and fell upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 30:2; Genesis 29:30–31; Genesis 45:22"> And when his armourbearer saw that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was dead, he fell likewise upon his sword, and died with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Job 6:14; Job 24:21; Leviticus 18:18"> So Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; died, and his three sons, and his armourbearer, and all his men, that same day together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="1 Samuel 2:19"> And when the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that &#91;were&#93; on the other side of the valley, and &#91;they&#93; that &#91;were&#93; on the other side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, saw that the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; fled, and that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and his sons were dead, they forsook the cities, and fled; and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; came and dwelt in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ruth 4:15; Job 6:14; Isaiah 54:6"> And it came to pass on the morrow, when the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; came to strip the slain, that they found Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and his three sons fallen in mount Gilboa &#91;swollen heap&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="1 Samuel 3:3; Tehillim - Psalms 27:4; Tehillim - Psalms 5:7"> And they cut off his head, and stripped off his armour, and sent into the land of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; round about, to publish &#91;it in&#93; the house of their idols, and among the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Job 7:11; Luke 22:44; Job 10:1"> And they put his armour in the house of ‘Ashtarot &#91;Star (female deity)&#93;: and they fastened his body to the wall of Beit-Sh’an &#91;house of ease&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Judges 13:5; Numbers 6:5; Genesis 29:32"> And when the inhabitants of Yavesh-Gil’ad &#91;Dry Hill of Testimony&#93; heard of that which the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; had done to Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="1 Thessalonians 5:17; Jacob 5:16; Luke 18:1"> All the valiant men arose, and went all night, and took the body of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and the bodies of his sons from the wall of Beit-Sh’an &#91;house of ease&#93;, and came to Yavesh &#91;dry&#93;, and burnt them there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Romans 8:26; Acts 2:13; Genesis 24:42–45"> And they took their bones, and buried &#91;them&#93; under a tree at Yavesh &#91;dry&#93;, and fasted seven days.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
